retires older ones. Applications relying on Anthropic models may need occasional updates to keep working. Impacted customers will always be notified by email and in the documentation.</p>
<p>This page lists all API deprecations, along with recommended replacements.</p>
<h2 id="overview" class="group">Overview<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_g5phinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic uses the following terms to describe the model lifecycle:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Active:</strong> The model is fully supported and recommended for use.</li>
<li><strong>Legacy:</strong> The model will no longer receive updates and may be deprecated in the future.</li>
<li><strong>Deprecated:</strong> The model is still functional but no longer recommended. Anthropic provides a recommended replacement and assigns a retirement date.</li>
<li><strong>Retired:</strong> The model is no longer available for use. Requests to retired models will fail.</li>
</ul>
<aside data-cds="Aside" data-icon-layout="first-line" role="note" class="flex items-start gap-xs rounded-card px-lg py-md font-sans text-body bg-warning text-warning shadow-ring-warning prose-block [.prose_&amp;]:text-prose"><span class="flex min-h-[1lh] shrink-0 items-center"><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:20px;font-weight:433.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span><div class="min-w-0 flex-1"><p>Deprecated models are likely to be less reliable than active models. Move workloads to active models to maintain the highest level of support and reliability.</p></div></aside>
<p>The dates on this page apply to Anthropic-operated platforms: the Claude API, <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-platform-on-aws">Claude Platform on AWS</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-microsoft-foundry">Microsoft Foundry</a>. Partner-operated platforms (Amazon Bedrock and Google Cloud) set their own retirement schedules, so a model&#x27;s lifecycle status and dates can differ. See the <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-in-amazon-bedrock#supported-models">Amazon Bedrock</a>, <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-on-amazon-bedrock-legacy#api-model-ids">Amazon Bedrock (Opus 4.6 and earlier)</a>, and <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/claude-on-vertex-ai#api-model-ids">Google Cloud</a> model tables.</p>
<h2 id="migrating-to-replacements" class="group">Migrating to replacements<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_gfphinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Once a model is deprecated, migrate all usage to a suitable replacement before the retirement date. Requests to models past the retirement date will fail.</p>
<p>To help measure the performance of replacement models on your tasks, consider thorough testing of your applications with the new models well before the retirement date.</p>
<p>For specific instructions on migrating to the latest Claude models, see the <a href="/docs/en/about-claude/models/migration-guide">Migration guide</a>.</p>
<h2 id="notifications" class="group">Notifications<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_gnphinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic notifies customers with active deployments for models with upcoming retirements, providing at least 60 days&#x27; notice before model retirement for publicly released models.</p>
<h2 id="auditing-model-usage" class="group">Auditing model usage<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_grphinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>To help identify usage of deprecated models, customers can access an audit of their API usage. Follow these steps:</p>
<ol>
<li>Go to the <a href="/usage">Usage</a> page in Claude Console.</li>
<li>Click <strong>Export</strong>.</li>
<li>Review the downloaded CSV to see usage broken down by API key and model.</li>
</ol>
<p>This audit will help you locate any instances where your application is still using deprecated models, allowing you to prioritize updates to newer models before the retirement date.</p>
<h2 id="best-practices" class="group">Best practices<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_h3phinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<ol>
<li>Regularly check the documentation for updates on model deprecations.</li>
<li>Test your applications with newer models well before the retirement date of your current model.</li>
<li>Update your code to use the recommended replacement model as soon as possible.</li>
<li>Contact the support team if you need assistance with migration or have any questions.</li>
</ol>
<h2 id="deprecation-downsides-and-mitigations" class="group">Deprecation downsides and mitigations<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_h7phinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic currently deprecates and retires models to ensure capacity for new model releases. This comes with downsides:</p>
<ul>
<li>Users who value specific models must migrate to new versions</li>
<li>Researchers lose access to models for ongoing and comparative studies</li>
<li>Model retirement introduces safety- and model welfare-related risks</li>
</ul>
<p>At some point, Anthropic hopes to make past models publicly available again. In the meantime, Anthropic has committed to long-term preservation of model weights and other measures to help mitigate these impacts. For more details, see <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/deprecation-commitments" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commitments on Model Deprecation and Preservation</a>.</p>

<h2 id="api-parameter-deprecations" class="group">API parameter deprecations<span class="relative hidden h-[1em] w-0 align-middle lg:inline-block"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" data-size="xs" class="cds-reset group/btn isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none cursor-[var(--cds-cursor-interactive)] aria-disabled:cursor-default data-[disabled]:cursor-default border-0 outline-none focus-visible:outline-hidden [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:outline-hidden rounded h-control font-sans text-body [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-disabled disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast text-primary font-normal aria-pressed:text-accent focus-visible:shadow-focus [&amp;[data-initial-focus]:focus]:shadow-focus aspect-square w-control px-0 absolute left-xs top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" aria-label="Copy link to clipboard" id="_R_k3phinpfl5tpfibrad4plb_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] inset-0 cds-btn-squish"><span data-cds-part="paint" class="absolute inset-0 rounded-[inherit] transition-[background-color,box-shadow,color] duration-fast ease-out group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] group-[[data-initial-focus]:focus]/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-[[aria-haspopup][aria-expanded=true]]/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent "></span></span><span class="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" style="font-family:var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);font-feature-settings:&quot;liga&quot; 0;font-optical-sizing:auto;font-style:normal;font-variation-settings:normal;line-height:1;width:1em;height:1em;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;user-select:none;font-size:16px;font-weight:533.3" aria-hidden="true"></span></span></button></span></h2>
<p>Anthropic occasionally deprecates request parameters that no longer apply to current models. How the API treats a deprecated parameter depends on the model, as the following table shows. Most SDKs keep deprecated parameters in their request types so existing code continues to type-check. The Python SDK (v1.0 and later) removes <code>temperature</code>, <code>top_p</code>, and <code>top_k</code>, so passing them raises a <code>TypeError</code>.</p>
<div class="prose-scroll text-body"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>Status</th><th>Behavior</th><th>Recommended replacement</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class=""><code>temperature</code>, <code>top_p</code>, <code>top_k</code></td><td class="">Deprecated (Claude Opus 4.7 and later)</td><td class="">Returns a 400 error when set to a non-default value on Claude 4.7 and later models and <a href="https://anthropic.com/glasswing" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Claude Mythos Preview</a>.</td><td class="">Omit and use <a href="/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-engineering/claude-prompting-best-practices">prompting</a> to guide model behavior.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>For migration steps, see the <a href="/docs/en/about-claude/models/migration-guide">migration guide</a>.</p></div><div class="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-sm border-t pt-6"><p class="text-secondary text-body font-medium">Was this page helpful?</p><div class="flex items-center gap-xs"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" data-cds-icon-only="" data-cds-ghost="" class="cds-reset group/btn
